Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The recognition variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 848 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 754 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on recognition structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 848. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 754. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on recognition structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 696. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 737 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the recognition space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 696 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 737. QED.
